<h2 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="inference"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#inference" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="18" height="18" viewBox="0 0 18 18" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true" class="size-3 shrink-0"><path d="M8.50001 6.827C8.14801 6.995 7.81801 7.225 7.52701 7.517L7.51701 7.527C6.13601 8.908 6.13601 11.146 7.51701 12.527L9.69201 14.702C11.073 16.083 13.311 16.083 14.692 14.702L14.702 14.692C16.083 13.311 16.083 11.073 14.702 9.692L13.771 8.761" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path><path d="M9.50002 11.173C9.85202 11.005 10.182 10.775 10.473 10.483L10.483 10.473C11.864 9.092 11.864 6.854 10.483 5.473L8.30802 3.298C6.92702 1.917 4.68902 1.917 3.30802 3.298L3.29802 3.308C1.91702 4.689 1.91702 6.927 3.29802 8.308L4.22902 9.239" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path></svg></div></a></div><span class="cursor-pointer">Inference</span></h2>
<span data-as="p">Use the Inference API to generate vector embeddings and rerank results using <a class="link" href="/guides/index-data/create-an-index#embedding-models">embedding models</a> and <a class="link" href="/guides/search/rerank-results#reranking-models">reranking models</a> hosted on Pinecone’s infrastructure.</span>
<span data-as="p">There are two ways to use the Inference API:</span>
<ul>
<li>As a standalone service, through the <a class="link" href="/reference/api/latest/inference/rerank">Rerank documents</a> and <a class="link" href="/reference/api/latest/inference/generate-embeddings">Generate vectors</a> endpoints.</li>
<li>As an integrated part of database operations, through the <a class="link" href="/reference/api/latest/control-plane/create_for_model">Create an index with integrated embedding</a>, <a class="link" href="/reference/api/latest/data-plane/upsert_records">Upsert text</a>, and <a class="link" href="/reference/api/latest/data-plane/search_records">Search with text</a> endpoints.</li>
</ul>
<span data-as="p">The following Pinecone SDKs support using the Inference API:</span>
